GREEN: (only if RED found a gap) fix per section 4 touch points.\n3. Bookkeeping: matrix disposition + notebook + ROADMAP + USER_GUIDE.\n"}], "eval-2-cmderr-playbook-with_skill-run-1": [{"name": "playbook.md", "type": "text", "content": "# cmderr=3 at resume on a fresh engine: diagnosis + fix playbook\n\n## Diagnosis (short answer)\n\nA Program-Buffer batch faulted on the halted hart: the hart raised an\nexception (cmderr=3 = \"exception\" in the RISC-V Debug Spec, here an\nillegal instruction) while executing instructions the hybrid transport\nplaced in the Debug Module's Program Buffer. On a FRESHLY CONFIGURED\nengine the concrete cause is almost always one of two things, and both\nfire during the reset-baseline capture that runs at the very start of\nresume:\n\n1. **The batched CSR sweep hit a caps over-claimed CSR.** The caps map\n   (`andes_caps.hpp` + `andes_caps_config.hpp`) claims the new engine\n   implements a CSR its RTL does not have. The drain-side sweep executes\n   `csrr` on it in the Program Buffer, the hart raises illegal\n   instruction, and the whole batch aborts. This is the expected failure\n   mode of a new engine config: the caps gates were tuned for the old\n   engines and the new config.inc turns a different set of `_NDS_*`\n   knobs on.\n\n2. **mstatus.FS/VS is Off (or WARL-pinned off) for the FPR/V sweeps.**\n   The capture and resume enable FS/VS before any `fld`/`fsd`/`vsetvl`/\n   `vle8.v`/`vse8.v` Program-Buffer execution. If the new engine has no\n   F/D or no V extension at all (while the QEMU leg drained FP/V state\n   and set `HYBRID_FLAG_FP`/`HYBRID_FLAG_V`), the mstatus enable write\n   pins FS/VS to 0 and the very first FP/V instruction in the Program\n   Buffer raises illegal instruction -> cmderr=3.\n\nWhy \"at resume\" on a fresh engine specifically: the reset-baseline cache\nkey includes the sim binary's mtime and size\n(`vsim src/hybrid/reset_baseline.hpp:26-35`), so a freshly built engine\nALWAYS misses the cache. On a miss, `apply_resume_from_qemu` captures the\nbaseline first (`vsim src/simulator.hpp:217-238`): it halts the freshly\nreset hart and runs a FULL drain with the CSR sweep enabled\n(`capture_reset_baseline`, `src/simulator.hpp:349-393`,\n`drain.set_csr_sweep(true)` at line 358). That sweep rides the batched\n`csrr`+`sd` Program Buffer path over the COMPLETE caps-supported CSR\nlist -- so one over-claimed CSR faults the batch before the restore even\nstarts. The exception escapes to the top-level catch in\n`vsim src/main.cpp:544`, which prints the `[critical] fatal exception:`\nline you saw.\n\nThe raw message string comes from `exec_progbuf` in\n`vsim src/hybrid/program_buffer.hpp:201-205`. Deliberate design: no\ncaller may silently continue on a PB fault -- the hart would be left\npartially restored/drained, which is worse than aborting.\n\n## Step 0: read the error message you actually have\n\nThe CSR sweep bisects its own faults. On a batch fault, `csr_sweep`\nre-runs the batch one CSR at a time and throws a message that NAMES the\noffender (`program_buffer.hpp:314-336`):\n\n```\ncsr_sweep: CSR 0x7d1 raised cmderr -- the caps map over-claims a CSR\nthe hart does not implement (fix andes_caps)\n```\n\nSo check the full log first:\n\n- **You see the bisected `csr_sweep: CSR 0x...` message** -> caps\n  over-claim confirmed, and the offending CSR is named for you. Go to\n  Step 2.\n- **You see only the raw `exec_progbuf: PB execution raised cmderr=3`**\n  -> the fault is in a Program-Buffer path OUTSIDE the CSR sweep's\n  bisect. On a fresh engine, in order of likelihood:\n  - the FPR `fsd`/`fld` block sweep or the V `vsetvl`/`vse8.v`/`vle8.v`\n    sweep, with FS/VS off or the extension absent from the RTL\n    (capture: `state_drain.hpp` lines 95/109/263; resume:\n    `resume_driver.hpp` lines 227/244, `pb_block_sweep` /\n    `pb_v_data_sweep` in `program_buffer.hpp`);\n  - a per-CSR PB `csrrw` fallback for a must-restore CSR\n    (`write_csr_abstract_or_pb`, `resume_driver.hpp:452-461` -- used for\n    mstatus, fcsr, vstart/vxsat/vxrm/vcsr, dcsr, mcycle, minstret): if\n    the abstract write faults AND the PB csrrw faults, the CSR really\n    does not exist on this hart -- for the V CSRs that means the engine\n    has no V while the handoff carries V state;\n  - rare: the sweep's batch faulted but no single CSR reproduced it\n    (the `throw;` at `program_buffer.hpp:335`).\n\n  Either way, Step 1 tells you what the engine really implements.\n\n## Step 1: establish ground truth (the engine's build config)\n\nThe engine's compiled-in config is the ONLY truth about what it\nimplements. Do not trust QEMU: its gdbstub advertises CSRs the vsim RTL\ndoes not implement (QEMU models a fuller core).\n\n```sh\n# the BUILD directory copy, not the external/ source tree\nless $VSIM_BIN_DIR/V<engine>/obj_sc/config.inc\n$VSIM_BIN_DIR/sim_<engine> --describe\n```\n\nFrom config.inc, note the `_NDS_*` knobs relevant to the fault:\n- privilege: `NDS_NUM_PRIVILEGE_LEVELS` (1 = M-only, 2 = M+U, 3 = M+S+U)\n- features: DSP / CODENSE / POWERBRAKE / STACKSAFE / ECC / PMA / ILM /\n  DLM / PERF_MONITOR / TRIGGER / CLIC / SMSTATEEN / CCTL / PPI / FIO ...\n- extensions: does the RTL have F/D? V? (this decides the FS/VS branch)\n\nIf the fault is the FS/VS branch (raw message during an FPR/V sweep and\nconfig.inc shows no F/D or no V): the engine cannot receive FP/V state\nat all. That is not a caps bug -- pair the engine with a matching QEMU\n`-cpu` (so the plugin does not set `HYBRID_FLAG_FP`/`HYBRID_FLAG_V` for\nstate the target cannot hold) or pick an engine that implements the\nextensions the workload uses. The rest of this playbook covers the caps\nover-claim, which is the common case.\n\n## Step 2: confirm the over-claim\n\nThree files, three roles (all in the vsim repo):\n\n| File | Role |\n|------|------|\n| `src/hybrid/andes_caps.hpp` | CSR address -> `AndesGate` (spec knowledge) |\n| `src/hybrid/andes_caps_config.hpp` | `AndesGate` -> `_NDS_*` macro binding |\n| `build/V<engine>/obj_sc/config.inc` | what THIS engine actually has |\n\nLook up the named CSR's `case` in `andes_gate_for()`\n(`andes_caps.hpp:60-116`). Two traps to check:\n\n- **No case at all** -> the CSR defaults to `ALWAYS` = transported\n  unconditionally. Every CSR in the ABI X-macro lists needs a deliberate\n  gate decision; ALWAYS is only for base machine CSRs every Andes core\n  has (mxstatus, mclk_ctl, mdcause).\n- **A case bound to the wrong feature** -> the gate is on for this\n  engine but the CSR still does not exist, because the gate encodes a\n  plausibly-related feature instead of the spec's exact existence\n  condition.\n\nCross-check against config.inc: is the gate's `_NDS_*` macro (see\n`andes_caps_config.hpp`) on for this engine while the hart faults the\nCSR? Then the gate is wrong.\n\n## Step 3: get the spec's existence condition (IM Requirement)\n\nThe gate must encode the SPEC's existence condition, exactly -- not a\nfeature that usually comes along with it. Pull it from the AndeStar V5\nSPA PDF (`AndeStar_V5_SPA_UM164_V1.7.pdf` in the andesim repo): each CSR\nsection has an `IM Requirement:` line, and \"Table 13: Existence\nconfiguration condition(s)\" is the consolidated cross-check. See the\nskill reference `references/spec-mining.md` for the extraction recipe\n(PyMuPDF dump + grep), and `scripts/extract_csr_inventory.py` /\n`tests/andes_csr/csr_inventory.csv` in the andesim repo for the\ncommitted inventory.\n\nTwo precedents, each a real bug this exact fault exposed:\n\n- `mcountermask_m` (0x7D1): exists when\n  `(mmsc_cfg.PMNDS==1) & (misa[20]==1)` -- BOTH perf monitor AND U-mode.\n  It was gated PERF_MONITOR only; on the M-only premium\n  (`NDS_NUM_PRIVILEGE_LEVELS=1`, perf monitor ON) the caps over-claimed\n  it and the PB `csrr` faulted. When a condition needs both a feature\n  and a privilege level, the privilege gate is the binding one for\n  current engines -- it is now UMODE, matching its twins 0x7D3/0x7CE.\n- `jvt` (0x017): Zcmt's table-jump base, NOT part of classic CoDense\n  (`exec.it` + `uitb`). Gating it CODENSE faulted the sweep on every\n  CoDense core. It is now ZCMT, bound to `_NDS_ZCMT_SUPPORT_INT` --\n  a macro no current config carries, so the gate reads as off, and a\n  future config turns it on without a code change.\n\n## Step 4: fix the gate, test-first (TDD)\n\n1. **RED**: in `vsim tests/hybrid/andes_caps.test.cpp`, model the NEW\n   engine's config.inc shape as a helper -- copy the `premium()` pattern\n   (line 22): `AndesCaps::none()` plus exactly the gates this engine's\n   `_NDS_*` macros turn on. Assert the expected `supports_csr()`\n   verdicts, including `CHECK_FALSE(c.supports_csr(0x...))` for the CSR\n   that faulted. Build and run in the vsim container; show the failure:\n\n   ```sh\n   podman run --rm -v /home/nick/work/vsim_andesim:/work -w /work \\\n     localhost/vsim-env:latest \\\n     bash -c 'cmake --build build -j && ctest --test-dir build -LE hybrid-e2e'\n   ```\n\n2. **GREEN**: move/add the `case` in `andes_gate_for()`\n   (`andes_caps.hpp`), and if the gate is new, add its `_NDS_*` binding\n   in `andes_caps_config.hpp` (an absent macro reads as \"off\", never a\n   compile error). Comment the spec condition next to the case, the way\n   0x7D1 and 0x017 are commented today. Rebuild the sim target; rerun\n   the unit suite green.\n\nThe test helper IS the machine-checked record of the engine shape --\nkeep it honest against config.inc.\n\n## Step 5: validate with a cold-cache rerun\n\nThe baseline capture is the caps validator: its CSR block rides the\nbatched PB sweep over the COMPLETE caps-supported list, so a clean\ncapture empirically proves every claimed CSR is `csrr`-able on this RTL.\nForce it to re-run:\n\n```sh\nrm -rf ~/.cache/andesim   # cache keys on binary mtime+size, but be explicit\n./andesim run --mode hybrid --trigger marker --engine vsim:<engine> \\\n  --verify tests/fixtures/rt_c_hello.elf\n```\n\n- Another over-claim -> another bisected `csr_sweep: CSR 0x...` abort.\n  Repeat Steps 2-4 for the named CSR until the capture is clean.\n- **Expect the `--verify` field count to DROP by one per de-claimed\n  CSR.** That is correct, not a regression: those fields were\n  zero-vs-zero false matches before (the tolerant abstract path mirrors\n  a faulting read as zero, and QEMU drains zero for a CSR the program\n  never wrote). Precedent: premium went 146 -> 144 when 0x7D1 and 0x017\n  left the set.\n\nThen climb the rest of the ladder:\n\n```sh\n# coverage guard (andesim repo): inventory == matrix == ABI\nbash tests/andes_csr/test_csr_coverage_complete.sh\n\n# full e2e matrix\nQEMU_BIN_DIR=... VSIM_BIN_DIR=... VSIM_QEMU=... VSIM_QEMU32=... \\\nVSIM_PLUGIN=/home/nick/work/andesim/qemu_plugin/libhybrid_handoff.so \\\nJOBS=8 bash scripts/run_e2e.sh\n```\n\n## Step 6: commit and document\n\nTidy First: the caps fix (behavioral) and any test-helper reshaping\n(structural) are separate commits, each on a green suite. Update the\nvsim `ROADMAP.md` known-limits and andesim `docs/USER_GUIDE.md` section\n4.4 (what `--verify` compares) if the compared set changed. If the\ncoverage matrix changed, regenerate `tests/andes_csr/csr_coverage.csv`\nthrough `scripts/classify_csr_coverage.py` -- never by hand.\n\n## What NOT to do\n\n- **Do not disable the CSR sweep** (`set_csr_sweep(false)` /\n  `set_block_store_drain(false)`) to make the run pass. The tolerant\n  per-CSR abstract path MASKS over-claims: a faulting read mirrors as\n  zero, QEMU drains zero, `--verify` matches zero == zero. Both\n  precedent bugs were \"verified\" for months this way. The batched sweep\n  is the only real caps validator.\n- **Do not use `--no-reset-baseline` as the fix.** It skips the liveness\n  gate (and the capture), hiding the fault on this run -- the same\n  over-claim still faults any full drain with the sweep on, and you lose\n  the elided-restore speedup.\n- **Do not wrap the fault in a try/catch or \"tolerate\" the cmderr.**\n  `exec_progbuf` throws by design: a partially restored/drained hart is\n  worse than an aborted run, and the fault is a config bug with a\n  one-line proper fix.\n- **Do not add the CSR to `andes_verify_excluded`**\n  (andesim `driver/state_diff.cpp`). Verify exclusion is only for\n  engine-reset divergences on CSRs the program never writes (mip timer\n  bit, msp_bound/msp_base, milmb/mdlmb enable) -- never to hide a\n  transport or caps bug.\n\n## Key files (for reference)\n\nvsim repo (`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim`):\n- `src/hybrid/program_buffer.hpp` -- `exec_progbuf` raw throw (201-205),\n  `csr_sweep` bisect (314-336), `pb_block_sweep`, `pb_v_data_sweep`\n- `src/simulator.hpp` -- baseline cache miss -> capture (217-238),\n  `capture_reset_baseline` with `set_csr_sweep(true)` + FS/VS enable\n  (349-393), restore-sweep-stays-off NOTE (266-272)\n- `src/hybrid/reset_baseline.hpp` -- cache key: engine + binary\n  mtime/size + ELF (fresh build always misses)\n- `src/hybrid/andes_caps.hpp` -- `andes_gate_for` address -> gate map\n- `src/hybrid/andes_caps_config.hpp` -- gate -> `_NDS_*` binding\n- `src/hybrid/resume_driver.hpp` -- restore order, `write_csr_abstract_or_pb`\n- `tests/hybrid/andes_caps.test.cpp` -- engine-shape helpers (`premium()`)\n- `build/V<engine>/obj_sc/config.inc` -- ground truth for the engine\n- `src/main.cpp:544` -- the `[critical] fatal exception` catch site\n\nandesim repo (`/home/nick/work/andesim`):\n- `tests/andes_csr/` -- inventory, coverage matrix, guard\n- `driver/state_diff.cpp` -- `--verify` compare + exclusions\n- `include/hybrid/state_abi.h` -- the X-macro CSR lists (wire format)\n"}], "eval-2-cmderr-playbook-without_skill-run-1": [{"name": "playbook.md", "type": "text", "content": "# Playbook: hybrid resume dies with `exec_progbuf: PB execution raised cmderr=3`\n\nScope: a hybrid run (`./andesim run --mode hybrid`) on a freshly configured /\nfreshly built vsim engine aborts in the accurate leg with:\n\n```\n[critical] fatal exception: exec_progbuf: PB execution raised cmderr=3\n```\n\nRepos referenced:\n- driver / plugin / harness: `/home/nick/work/andesim`\n- vsim engine + hybrid orchestration: `/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim`\n\n---\n\n## 1. What the message means\n\nThe thrower is `exec_progbuf()` in\n`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/program_buffer.hpp` (line 201-205).\nIt writes a short instruction sequence into the RISC-V Debug Module **Program\nBuffer**, tells the halted hart to execute it, then reads back\n`abstractcs.cmderr`. The exception escapes to the `catch` in\n`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/src/main.cpp:543` which prints the\n`[critical] fatal exception:` line and exits 1 (so the driver then reports a\n\"plan step 1: expected exit 200, got 1\" style error).\n\n`cmderr` is the RISC-V Debug Spec abstract-command error code:\n\n| cmderr | meaning |\n|--------|---------|\n| 1 | busy |\n| 2 | not supported |\n| 3 | **exception - the hart took an architectural exception while executing the injected instructions** |\n| 4 | halt/resume |\n| 5 | bus error |\n\nSo cmderr=3 means: one of the instructions the resume flow injected into the\nProgram Buffer **trapped inside the hart**. In this flow only two trap classes\noccur in practice:\n\n1. **Illegal instruction** - the instruction touches an extension or CSR the\n   engine's RTL does not implement (no FPU -> `fld`/`fsd` trap; no V ->\n   `vsetvl`/`vle8.v`/`vse8.v` trap; `csrrw` to an unimplemented CSR traps), or\n   the extension is off in `mstatus` (FS/VS = Off, which on a core *without*\n   the extension is WARL-pinned to Off, so the enable write silently no-ops).\n2. **Load/store access fault** - the sweep's `ld`/`sd`/`fld`/`fsd`/\n   `vle8.v`/`vse8.v` addresses the shared-mmap scratch page at a guest\n   physical address the engine's memory decode does not map.\n\n**Important discriminator built into the code:** the Andes/M-ext CSR sweep\n(`csr_sweep()` in the same header) catches a faulting batch, bisects it one\nCSR at a time, and rethrows a *named* error:\n`csr_sweep: CSR 0x... raised cmderr -- the caps map over-claims a CSR ...`.\nTherefore, if you see the **raw** `exec_progbuf: PB execution raised cmderr=3`\nmessage, the fault is **NOT** in the Andes/M-ext CSR list. It is one of the\nfixed Program-Buffer sequences: the GPR/FPR block sweeps, the V data sweep,\nthe Tier-B `vsetvl`, or a single `csrrw` fallback.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Where it can throw, in run order\n\nA first hybrid run on a fresh engine executes, inside the one vsim process,\nin this order (`Simulator::apply_resume_from_qemu`,\n`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/src/simulator.hpp:208`):\n\n**Phase 0 - LG3 reset-baseline capture** (runs *before* resume; a freshly\nbuilt engine binary always misses the `$XDG_CACHE_HOME/andesim` cache, so this\nalways runs on the first hybrid run of a new build):\n\n| PB sequence | instructions | traps when |\n|---|---|---|\n| GPR block store | `sd x1..x30, off(x31)` | scratch page not mapped/writable |\n| FPR block store (if state FLAG_FP) | `fsd f0..f31, off(x5)` | **engine has no FPU** (FS pinned Off -> illegal insn), or scratch fault |\n| Andes+M-ext CSR sweep | `csrr` + `sd` per CSR | caps over-claim - but this one throws the NAMED `csr_sweep: CSR 0x...` error, not the raw one |\n| V dump sweep (if FLAG_V) | `vsetvl` then `vse8.v v0..v24` | **engine has no V** (illegal insn), or scratch fault |\n\n**Phase 1 - `ResumeDriver::resume()`**\n(`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/resume_driver.hpp:146`):\n\n| step | PB sequence | traps when |\n|---|---|---|\n| FPR reload (FLAG_FP) | `fld f0..f31, off(x5)` | no FPU / scratch fault |\n| fcsr, vstart, vxsat, vxrm, vcsr, mcycle, minstret | abstract write first; on abstract cmderr, PB fallback `csrrw x0, csr, x5` | the CSR does not exist on this engine (FP/V CSRs on a no-FP/no-V core) |\n| V reload (FLAG_V) | `vsetvl` + `vle8.v v0..v24` | no V / scratch fault |\n| Tier-B vtype+vl | `vsetvl x0, x5, x6` | no V |\n| GPR reload | `ld x1..x31, off(x31)` | scratch fault |\n| Andes+M-ext restore | abstract tolerant writes only | never throws (clears cmderr per CSR) |\n\nNote: both phases print the identical `[critical]` line, and the andesim\ndriver *hides* vsim's `[info]` log lines (commit b86d168), so from the driver\nyou cannot tell which phase died. Section 4 shows how to tell.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Root causes on a freshly configured engine, ranked\n\n### RC1 (by far the most common): QEMU fast leg models MORE ISA than the new engine\n\nThe QEMU plugin (`/home/nick/work/andesim/qemu_plugin/hybrid_handoff.c`,\n`drain_state_into`) sets `HYBRID_FLAG_FP` / `HYBRID_FLAG_V` when the QEMU\n*CPU model* advertises the FPU / V registers - **regardless of whether the\nworkload used them**. So even a pure scalar ELF drains FP + V state. The\nresume then executes `fld` / `vsetvl` / `vle8.v` on an engine whose RTL has no\nFPU / no V -> illegal instruction -> cmderr=3.\n\nThe framework's ISA-sync mechanism is: the driver probes the engine with\n`sim_<cfg> --print-qemu-config` and passes the result to QEMU via\n`-readconfig` (`/home/nick/work/andesim/driver/run_plan.cpp:248-255,184-187`).\nThree concrete ways a *fresh* engine breaks this chain:\n\n1. **Empty qemu.cfg.** The cfg is generated at engine-build time by\n   `external/cpu_config_to_march_converter/scripts/config_inc_to_qemu_cfg.py`\n   (`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/cmake/VerilatorHelpers.cmake:148-164`). If\n   that submodule was not initialized when you configured the new engine,\n   cmake embeds an **empty** qemu.cfg (\"No CPU_CONFIG_QEMU_CONVERTER\"), the\n   driver's probe returns empty, and the fast leg silently falls back to the\n   pinned default `-cpu andes-ax45mpv,vlen=<engine vlen|512>` - full FP + V.\n\n2. **Driver-name family mismatch.** qemu.cfg's `[global]` blocks target\n   `driver = \"andes-<family>-riscv-cpu\"` where `<family>` is the engine name\n   up to the first `_` (`ax45mpv_premium -> ax45mpv`,\n   VerilatorHelpers.cmake:147). But the hybrid fast leg **hardcodes**\n   `-cpu andes-ax45mpv,vlen={vlen}`\n   (`/home/nick/work/andesim/driver/engine_registry.cpp:52-53`). For any\n   engine whose family is not `ax45mpv` (e.g. a fresh `d25`, `ax66_...`,\n   `nx45v_...`), the globals name a device class QEMU never instantiates and\n   are **silently ignored** - QEMU still runs full ax45mpv ISA.\n\n3. **The converter is \"on\"-only.** `config_inc_to_qemu_cfg.py`\n   (`_collect_isa_props`) emits `value = \"on\"` for extensions present in the\n   config; it never emits `off` for absent ones (only `zcd` is ever forced\n   off). An extension that is ON by default in the base QEMU model but absent\n   in your engine config is never disabled.\n\nNet effect: state file flags claim FP/V/CSRs the engine lacks -> first PB\ninstruction touching them traps.\n\n### RC2: scratch-page access fault (memory map mismatch)\n\nEvery block sweep moves data through the V-scratch region: the TOP page of the\nshared mmap, GPA = `mmap_base + physical_mmap_bytes(mem_size) - 4096`\n(`v_scratch_gpa`, `/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/v_regfile_xfer.hpp`).\nThe driver passes `--shared-mem-base 0x0` and\n`--shared-mem-size = --mem-size + one page` (default 128 MiB + 4 KiB), and\nvsim maps that window as its only RAM (`main.cpp:389-397`). If the fresh\nengine's decode does not cover that window - an ILM/DLM whose base overlaps\nit, a PMA reset configuration that rejects it, or a narrower physical address\ndecode - the first `sd`/`ld`/`fld`/`vle8.v` to scratch takes an access fault\n-> cmderr=3.\n\n### RC3: caps-map over-claim (different signature - listed to rule it OUT)\n\n`andes_caps_from_config()`\n(`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/andes_caps_config.hpp`) derives the\ntransported-CSR set from the `_NDS_*` macros in the engine's **built**\n`config.inc` (memory note: the built engine's\n`build/V<cfg>/obj_sc/config.inc` is the ground truth, which can differ from\nthe `external/` source tree). If a fresh config spells a feature macro\ndifferently, or a CSR falls into `andes_gate_for()`'s `ALWAYS` default, the\nsweep transports a CSR the hart lacks. But that fails with the **named**\n`csr_sweep: CSR 0x...` error (drain/baseline-capture side) or, in the known\nworst case, a DMI **hang / \"timed out\"** (documented for `ucode`/`mecc_code`\non ax45mpv_premium in `andes_caps.hpp`'s header comment). A raw\n`exec_progbuf ... cmderr=3` is not this; check RC1/RC2 first.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Step-by-step diagnosis\n\nAll commands are read-only probes plus manual re-runs of the failing legs.\n\n**Step 1 - get the exact leg commands.**\n\n```sh\ncd /home/nick/work/andesim\n./andesim run --mode hybrid --engine verilator:<cfg> --trigger marker --dry-run <elf>\n```\n\nInspect the fast-leg argv:\n- Is `-readconfig <workdir>/qemu.cfg` present at all? Missing => RC1(1):\n  the engine's `--print-qemu-config` probe returned nothing.\n- What `-cpu` is pinned? (`andes-ax45mpv,vlen=...` today.)\n\n**Step 2 - interrogate the engine binary** (the built binary is the truth,\nnot the `external/` tree):\n\n```sh\nSIM=$VSIM_BIN_DIR/sim_<cfg>          # from config.env\n$SIM --describe                      # vlen/xlen + \"andes_csrs\" the caps gate claims\n$SIM --print-qemu-config             # MUST be non-empty; note the driver= family\n$SIM --print-config | grep -E 'NDS_(RVV|FPU|VLEN|ELEN|ILM|DLM|PMA|NUM_PRIVILEGE)'\n$SIM --print-memmap                  # RAM window the decode actually maps\n```\n\nDecision points:\n- `--print-qemu-config` empty -> RC1(1). Fix 1.\n- `driver = \"andes-<family>-riscv-cpu\"` family != the `-cpu` family in the\n  dry-run fast leg -> RC1(2). Fix 2.\n- config.inc has no `NDS_RVV_SUPPORT` / no FPU, but the base `-cpu` model has\n  V/FP on by default -> RC1(3). Fix 2/3.\n- `--print-memmap` does not cover `[0x0, mem_size + 4096)` or shows ILM/DLM\n  inside that window -> RC2. Fix 4.\n\n**Step 3 - confirm what the state file claims.** Run the dry-run's QEMU\nfast-leg command by hand (the driver deletes its workdir, so use your own\npaths for `outfile=` and the mmap), then read the flags word:\n\n```sh\nxxd -l 12 state-0.bin     # bytes 0-3 magic 'AHYB', 4-7 version, 8-11 flags\n```\n\nFlags (`/home/nick/work/andesim/include/hybrid/state_abi.h`): bit0 RV64,\nbit1 FP, bit2 V, bit3 ANDES, bit4 M_EXT. `flags = 0x1f` on a scalar workload\nwith a scalar engine confirms RC1 (QEMU over-advertised). A python decoder\nexists at `/home/nick/work/andesim/tools/orchestrator/abi.py`.\n\n**Step 4 - pinpoint the throwing phase.** Run the vsim accurate-leg command\nby hand (from the dry run) with two additions:\n\n```sh\n$SIM <elf> --resume-from-qemu state-0.bin ... --log-level 1\n```\n\n- No `hybrid: reset baseline ...` info line before the crash -> died in the\n  LG3 **baseline-capture drain** (phase 0). (The driver hides `[info]` lines;\n  running by hand shows them.)\n- Add `--no-reset-baseline` to skip phase 0 entirely; if the crash persists,\n  it is in `resume()` proper. Either way the root-cause set is the same\n  (section 2 tables), this just tells you which sweep fired first.\n\n**Step 5 - sanity-check the pairing itself.**\n- The ELF march must fit the engine: an `-march=rv64gcv` fixture cannot run on\n  a V-less engine in *any* mode. Cross-check with a standalone cycle run:\n  `./andesim run --mode cycle --engine verilator:<cfg> <elf>`.\n- A known-good control: the same hybrid command on `verilator:ax45mpv_premium`\n  should pass end to end; that isolates the problem to the new engine's\n  config/pairing, not the harness.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Fixes\n\n**Fix 1 - regenerate the ISA sync (empty qemu.cfg).**\nIn `/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim`:\n\n```sh\ngit submodule update --init external/cpu_config_to_march_converter\n./build_vsim.sh          # or re-run the cmake build for the engine target\n$VSIM_BIN_DIR/sim_<cfg> --print-qemu-config   # now non-empty\n```\n\nThe rebuilt binary embeds `build/V<cfg>/obj_sc/qemu.cfg`; the driver picks it\nup automatically on the next `andesim run` (no driver change needed).\n\n**Fix 2 - make the family match an instantiable QEMU model.**\nThe engine name's prefix before the first `_` must be a real Andes QEMU CPU\n(`qemu-system-riscv64 -cpu help | grep andes`), because both the qemu.cfg\n`driver=` name and (ideally) the fast leg's `-cpu` derive from it. If your\nfresh engine is not in the ax45mpv family, know that today the driver still\npins `-cpu andes-ax45mpv` for the hybrid fast leg\n(`driver/engine_registry.cpp:52-53`) - the qemu.cfg globals for another\nfamily will not apply. Options, in order of preference:\n- Framework fix: derive the fast-leg `-cpu` from the accurate engine's family\n  (same `REGEX REPLACE \"_.*$\"` rule) instead of hardcoding ax45mpv - this is\n  the real root-cause repair for non-ax45mpv engines.\n- Interim: reproduce the shell harness pairing manually - `tests/e2e.sh`\n  takes `QEMU_CPU=andes-<family>,vlen=<VLEN>` (see\n  `scripts/run_e2e.sh:123-124` for the ax66_makatau <-> andes-ax66,vlen=128\n  precedent) and runs the same two-leg flow.\n\n**Fix 3 - extension ON in the base model but absent in the engine.**\nThe converter never emits `off`, so pick a base QEMU model whose defaults are\na subset of the engine ISA (Fix 2 usually achieves this: the same-family\nmodel matches by construction), or extend\n`config_inc_to_qemu_cfg.py::_collect_isa_props` to force-off extensions the\nconfig lacks (`v`, `f`, `d` are the ones that bite: their state transport\nexecutes real instructions in the Program Buffer).\n\n**Fix 4 - memory map (RC2).**\nThe engine must map `[--shared-mem-base, base + mem_size + 4096)` as plain\nRAM in hybrid mode. Check the fresh config's `NDS_ILM_BASE` / `NDS_DLM_BASE`\ndo not fall inside the window (default window: `0x0 .. 0x08001000` for the\n128 MiB default), and `--print-memmap` shows the interconnect actually\ndecoding it. If the program needs the whole window, remember the top page is\nreserved for the transport scratch - the driver already rejects ELFs that\nreach into it, but a shrunken engine RAM window is the engine's problem to\nfix, not the driver's.\n\n**Fix 5 - caps map (only if the error is the NAMED csr_sweep variant, or a\npoll timeout/hang).**\nBind the new config.inc macro in\n`/home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/andes_caps_config.hpp`\n(absent macro reads as \"feature off\" - so a renamed macro silently drops the\ngate) and/or add the CSR's gate case in `andes_gate_for()`\n(`andes_caps.hpp` - an unlisted CSR defaults to ALWAYS = transported\nunconditionally). Validate offline without running anything:\n\n```sh\npython3 /home/nick/work/andesim/scripts/config_inc_to_andes_caps.py \\\n    /home/nick/work/vsim_andesim/build/V<cfg>/obj_sc/config.inc\n# must equal the \"andes_csrs\" list from `sim_<cfg> --describe`\n# drift guard: tests/andes_sim/test_andes_caps_parity.sh\n```\n\n**Verify the fix.**\n\n```sh\n./andesim run --mode hybrid --engine verilator:<cfg> --trigger marker --verify <elf>\nJOBS=8 bash scripts/run_e2e.sh        # regression sweep on the stock engine\n```\n\n`--verify` matters here: it keeps the full drain and diffs the round-trip, so\nan ISA fix that merely stops the crash but drops state (e.g. V silently off\non both sides now) is caught by the state diff, and a fresh reset-baseline is\ncaptured for the new binary automatically (cache key includes the sim\nbinary's mtime/size, so no stale-baseline cleanup is needed).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Quick reference\n\nFault-to-cause map for the raw error:\n\n| first faulting PB insn | likely cause |\n|---|---|\n| `fsd`/`fld` (FPR sweep) | state FLAG_FP set, engine has no FPU -> RC1 |\n| `vsetvl`, `vle8.v`, `vse8.v` | state FLAG_V set, engine has no V -> RC1 |\n| `csrrw x0, <csr>, x5` (fallback) | abstract write NAK'd then CSR truly absent (fcsr/vstart/... on no-FP/no-V core) -> RC1 |\n| `ld`/`sd` (GPR sweep) | scratch page unmapped/blocked -> RC2 |\n\nKey files:\n\n| file | role |\n|---|---|\n| `vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/program_buffer.hpp` | thrower; `exec_progbuf`, `pb_block_sweep`, `csr_sweep`, `pb_v_data_sweep` |\n| `vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/resume_driver.hpp` | resume order; `write_csr_abstract_or_pb` fallback |\n| `vsim_andesim/src/simulator.hpp` | `apply_resume_from_qemu`: baseline capture runs before resume |\n| `vsim_andesim/src/hybrid/andes_caps{,_config}.hpp` | CSR capability gates from config.inc |\n| `vsim_andesim/cmake/VerilatorHelpers.cmake` | qemu.cfg generation + family-name rule |\n| `andesim/driver/run_plan.cpp` | `--print-qemu-config` probe -> `-readconfig` |\n| `andesim/driver/engine_registry.cpp` | pinned hybrid fast-leg `-cpu andes-ax45mpv` |\n| `andesim/qemu_plugin/hybrid_handoff.c` | flags come from QEMU's advertised features, not the workload |\n"}]}, "benchmark": {"metadata": {"skill_name": "csr-transport-bringup", "skill_path": "<path/to/skill>", "executor_model": "<model-name>", "analyzer_model": "<model-name>", "timestamp": "2026-07-02T06:09:03Z", "evals_run": [0, 1, 2, 3], "runs_per_configuration": 3}, "runs": [{"eval_id": 0, "configuration": "old_skill", "run_number": 1, "result": {"pass_rate": 1.0, "passed": 5, "failed": 0, "total": 5, "time_seconds": 402.1, "tokens": 72987, "tool_calls": 0, "errors": 0}, "expectations": [{"text": "mcountermask_m existence stated as the COMPOUND condition PMNDS==1 AND misa[20]==1 (U-mode), not the perf-monitor feature alone", "passed": true, "evidence": "Section 9.6.2 quote: 'IM Requirement: mmsc_cfg.PMNDS==1 & misa[20]==1 (User mode implemented)' plus Table 13 group '(mmsc_cfg.PMNDS==1) & (misa[20]==1) // user';
